-------- Alter 901Company Details -------- 
 CREATE OR ALTER FUNCTION Aicon.fn_GetDefaultCompanyDetails()
RETURNS TABLE
AS
RETURN
(
    WITH Selector AS
    (
        -- 1) grab the raw setting
        SELECT TOP (1)
            DefaultCompanyId
        FROM Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails
        -- you could filter here if it’s per-user, per-tenant, etc.
    ),
    NormSelector AS
    (
        -- 2) normalize NULL/empty/1 → 1
        SELECT
            CASE
                WHEN DefaultCompanyId IS NULL
                  OR DefaultCompanyId = 1 THEN 1
                ELSE DefaultCompanyId
            END AS sel
        FROM Selector
    ),
    OrderedCompanies AS
    (
        -- 3) number your rows in natural order
        SELECT
            CompanyId,
            DefaultCompanyId,
            currencytype,
            ROW_NUMBER() OVER (ORDER BY CompanyId) AS rn
        FROM Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails
    )
    -- 4) pick out exactly the Nth row
    SELECT
        oc.CompanyId,
        oc.DefaultCompanyId,
        oc.currencytype
    FROM OrderedCompanies AS oc
    CROSS JOIN NormSelector    AS ns
    WHERE oc.rn = ns.sel
);
GO

-----------------------Custom Currency Defaults-----------------------

-- Set default value = 1 for CurrencyId
DECLARE @ConstraintName1 NVARCHAR(200);
SELECT @ConstraintName1 = dc.name
FROM sys.default_constraints dc
JOIN sys.columns c ON c.default_object_id = dc.object_id
WHERE OBJECT_NAME(dc.parent_object_id) = 'tbl201VoucherMaster'
  AND c.name = 'CurrencyId';

IF @ConstraintName1 IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
    EXEC('ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster DROP CONSTRAINT ' + QUOTENAME(@ConstraintName1));
    PRINT 'Dropped existing default on CurrencyId';
END

ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster
ADD CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201VoucherMaster_CurrencyId DEFAULT 1 FOR CurrencyId;
PRINT 'Default 1 set for CurrencyId';


-- Set default value = 1 for BaseCurrencyId
DECLARE @ConstraintName2 NVARCHAR(200);
SELECT @ConstraintName2 = dc.name
FROM sys.default_constraints dc
JOIN sys.columns c ON c.default_object_id = dc.object_id
WHERE OBJECT_NAME(dc.parent_object_id) = 'tbl201VoucherMaster'
  AND c.name = 'BaseCurrencyId';

IF @ConstraintName2 IS NOT NULL
BEGIN
    EXEC('ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster DROP CONSTRAINT ' + QUOTENAME(@ConstraintName2));
    PRINT 'Dropped existing default on BaseCurrencyId';
END

ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster
ADD CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201VoucherMaster_BaseCurrencyId DEFAULT 1 FOR BaseCurrencyId;
PRINT 'Default 1 set for BaseCurrencyId';

----------------------------User Access Web ---------------------------
CREATE TABLE Aicon.tblUserAccessWeb (  

[SlNo] [int] IDENTITY(1,1) NOT NULL,  
[UserID] [tinyint] NOT NULL,  
[ItemForm] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemName] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemDescription] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemEnabled] [bit] NULL,  
[ItemVisible] [bit] NULL,  
[ItemRemarks] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[Module] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[MasterSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[GroupSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[ItemSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[IsFullAccess] [bit] NULL,  
[ItemNotes] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ISWeb] [int] NULL,  

)  

CREATE TABLE Aicon.tbl901UserAccessMasterWeb(  
[SlNo] [int] NOT NULL,  
[ItemForm] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemName] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemDescription] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[ItemEnabled] [bit] NULL,  
[ItemVisible] [bit] NULL,  
[ItemRemarks] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[Module] [varchar](max) NULL,  
[MasterSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[GroupSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[ItemSlNo] [decimal](18, 0) NULL,  
[IsFullAccess] [bit] NULL,  
)  

  

create PROCEDURE [Aicon].[stPro901_04ReplicateUserAccessPermissionsWeb]    
(    
@FromUser tinyint,    
@ToUser tinyint    
)    
AS    
DELETE Aicon.tblUserAccessweb where UserID = @ToUser    

Insert into    Aicon.tblUserAccessweb    

(UserID, ItemForm, ItemName, ItemDescription, ItemEnabled, ItemVisible, ItemRemarks, Module,  MasterSlNo, GroupSlNo, ItemSlNo)    

select @ToUser, ItemForm, ItemName, ItemDescription, ItemEnabled, ItemVisible, ItemRemarks, Module,  MasterSlNo, GroupSlNo, ItemSlNo    
FROM         Aicon.tblUserAccessweb    

where UserID = @FromUser

	ALTER VIEW [Aicon].[qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster] 

AS 

SELECT Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherNo, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherDate, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherRefNo, ISNULL(Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherNarration, N'') AS VoucherNarration,  

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherEnteredBy, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherEnteredOn, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherVerifiedBy, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherVerifiedOn, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherApprovedBy,  

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherApprovedOn, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.VoucherEntryNo, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.AccountHead, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.AccountHeadName, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.DrCr,  

                  Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.DrAmount, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.CrAmount, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.VoucherAmountFormatted, ISNULL(Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.EntryNarration, N'') AS EntryNarration,  

                  Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroup, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroup, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.IsApproved, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherType, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.SysRemarks,  

                  Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.IsCalculateOpeningBalance, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.BankClearedOn, Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroupID, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroupID, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.IsProfitLossAccount,  

                  Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.IsBalanceSheetAccount, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterOrderNo, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroupCategory, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherEffectiveDate), 0)  

                  AS VoucherEffectiveDate, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.BillNo, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.BillDate, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.BillPaidTo, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.BillRemarks, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherModifiedBy,  

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherModifiedOn, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountHeadArabic, Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroupAr, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroupAr, Aicon.tbl201VoucherTypes.VoucherTypeAr,  

                  Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.ChartOfAccountsOrder, Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroupOrderNo, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroupCategoryAr, Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.IsAccumulatedDepAcc,  

                  Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountSubGroup, Aicon.tbl20123LedgerSubGroups.SubGroupName, Aicon.tbl20123LedgerSubGroups.SubGroupNameAr, Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MainGroup, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.ReferenceNote,  

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.Currencyid, Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.CurrencyRate 

FROM     Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup RIGHT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups ON Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup.MasterGroupID = Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroupUnder RIGHT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries LEFT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts LEFT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.tbl20123LedgerSubGroups ON Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountSubGroup = Aicon.tbl20123LedgerSubGroups.LedgerSubGroupCode ON Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.AccountHead = Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountID ON  

                  Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups.AccountGroupID = Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountGroupID RIGHT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherTypes RIGHT OUTER JOIN 

                  Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster ON Aicon.tbl201VoucherTypes.VoucherType = Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherType ON Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntries.VoucherNo = Aicon.tbl201VoucherMaster.VoucherNo 

GO 






 ALTER PROCEDURE [Aicon].[sp901_01UpdateLayout]      
(      
    @LayoutXML NVARCHAR(MAX),    
    @FormID VARCHAR(MAX),    
    @UserID VARCHAR(50),    
    @IsFromWeb BIT = 1  -- Default: TRUE  
)      
AS      
BEGIN  
    SET NOCOUNT ON;  
    IF EXISTS (  
        SELECT 1   
        FROM Aicon.tbl90111LayoutMaster   
        WHERE FormID = @FormID AND UserID = @UserID  
    )  
    BEGIN  
        -- Update existing layout  
        UPDATE Aicon.tbl90111LayoutMaster  
        SET LayoutXml = @LayoutXML  
        WHERE FormID = @FormID AND UserID = @UserID;  
    END  
    ELSE  
    BEGIN  
        -- Insert new layout record  
        INSERT INTO Aicon.tbl90111LayoutMaster (FormID, UserID, LayoutXml)  
        VALUES (@FormID, @UserID, @LayoutXML);  
    END  
END  




 



 
  

 sp_helptext sp_01totClientOutstanding


 -- Step 1: Create the table 

CREATE TABLE Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding ( 

    AccountHead NVARCHAR(100), 

    Balance DECIMAL(18, 2) 

); 

  

-- Procedure to calculate total Supplier Outstanding 

CREATE PROCEDURE Aicon.sp_01totSupOutstanding 

AS 

BEGIN 

    DELETE FROM Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding 

    WHERE AccountHead = 'supplier outstanding'; 

  

    INSERT INTO Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding (AccountHead, Balance) 

    SELECT 'supplier outstanding', SUM([Balance]) 

    FROM Aicon.qry20115BillsPayableOutStanding 

    WHERE Balance > 0; 

END; 

  

-- Procedure to calculate total Client Outstanding 

CREATE PROCEDURE Aicon.sp_01totClientOutstanding 

AS 

BEGIN 

    DELETE FROM Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding 

    WHERE AccountHead = 'Client outstanding'; 

  

    INSERT INTO Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding (AccountHead, Balance) 

    SELECT 'Client outstanding', SUM(Balance) 

    FROM Aicon.Qry20115BillsOutStanding 

    WHERE Balance > 0; 

END; 

  

-- Procedure to calculate total Cash Balance 

CREATE PROCEDURE Aicon.sp_01totcashbalance 

AS 

BEGIN 

    DELETE FROM Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding 

    WHERE AccountHead = 'Cash Balance'; 

  

    INSERT INTO Aicon.tbl01supplieroutstanding (AccountHead, Balance) 

    SELECT 'Cash balance', SUM(ISNULL(DrAmount, 0) - ISNULL(CrAmount, 0)) AS CashBalance 

    FROM Aicon.Qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster 

    WHERE AccountGroup IN ('BANK ACCOUNTS', 'CASH-IN-HAND'); 

END; 

  

-- Execute the procedures 

EXEC sp_01totClientOutstanding; 

EXEC sp_01totSupOutstanding; 

EXEC sp_01totcashbalance;

                      CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Dr' THEN VoucherAmount ELSE - VoucherAmount END AS VoucherAmountFormatted, SysRemarks,Type  
FROM         Aicon.tbl201VoucherEntry

		     Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.Balance * ISNULL(Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedBalance, 
			 Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.Received, 
			 Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.Balance,
			 Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.AccountGroup, 
             Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.InvoiceDueDate,
			 Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.OverdueDays, CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 0 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END AS NotOverdue, CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 0 THEN (CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 30 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END) 
             ELSE 0 END AS Less30, CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 30 THEN (CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 60 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END) ELSE 0 END AS Less30to60, CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 60 THEN (CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 90 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END) ELSE 0 END AS Less60to90, 
             CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 90 THEN (CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 180 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END) ELSE 0 END AS Less90to180, CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 180 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END AS More180, 
             CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 180 THEN (CASE WHEN OverdueDays <= 365 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END) ELSE 0 END AS Less180to365, CASE WHEN OverdueDays > 365 THEN Balance ELSE 0 END AS More365, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.SalesPersonName, 
             Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.Inv_SalesMan, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CostAllocationUnit, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CostAllocationGroup, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CostAllocationMasterGroup, 
             Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CompanyBranch, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.DivisionName, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.SubGroupName, Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.LedgerSubGroupCode, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountHeadArabic, 
             Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.VATRegistrationNo, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.ClientOtherID, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.NoOfDaysCreditPeriod, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountsContactName, Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountsContactMobile, 
             Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountsContactTitle,
			   Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.CurrencyName,Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.TransactionCurrencySymbol
FROM   Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing LEFT OUTER JOIN
             Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts ON Aicon.qry20104BillsReceivableAgeing.AccountHeadNo = Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountID

             CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Dr' THEN - AmountAllocated ELSE 0 END AS Expenses, 
			 CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Cr' THEN AmountAllocated ELSE 0 END AS Income, 
			 CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Dr' THEN - ConvertedAmountAllocated ELSE 0 END AS ConvertedExpenses, 
			 CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Cr' THEN ConvertedAmountAllocated ELSE 0 END AS ConvertedIncome,
			  CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Cr' THEN ConvertedAmountAllocated ELSE - ConvertedAmountAllocated END AS ConvertedCostAmount,
			 CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Cr' THEN 'Income' ELSE 'Expenses' END AS PL, 
             CASE WHEN DrCr = 'Cr' THEN AmountAllocated ELSE - AmountAllocated END AS CostAmount
			 , AccountID, VoucherNo, VoucherType, CostCenterIncharge, EntryNarration, SysRemarks, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, VoucherDate), 0) AS VoucherDate, ProjectMasterCode, 
             BranchCode, BranchName, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, VoucherEffectiveDate), 0) AS VoucherEffectiveDate, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, EffectiveDate), 0) AS AllocationEffectiveDate, VoucherNarration, VoucherRefNo
FROM   Aicon.qry20101CostAllocatedMaster

             AccountHead, AccountGroup, MasterGroup, Expenses, Income, PL, CostAmount, AccountID, VoucherNo, VoucherType, VoucherType + '::' + VoucherNo AS VoucherTypeAndNo, CostCenterIncharge, EntryNarration, SysRemarks, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, VoucherDate), 0) 
             AS VoucherDate, DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, VoucherDate), 0) AS VoucherMonth, DATEADD(year, DATEDIFF(year, 0, VoucherDate), 0) AS VoucherYear, DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, VoucherEffectiveDate), 0) AS EffectiveMonth, DATEADD(year, DATEDIFF(year, 
             0, VoucherEffectiveDate), 0) AS EffectiveYear, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, AllocationEffectiveDate), 0) AS AllocationEffectiveDate, DATEADD(month, DATEDIFF(month, 0, AllocationEffectiveDate), 0) AS AllocationEffectiveMonth, DATEADD(year, DATEDIFF(year, 0, 
             AllocationEffectiveDate), 0) AS AllocationEffectiveYear, ProjectMasterCode, BranchCode, BranchName, VoucherNarration, VoucherRefNo,ConvertedIncome,ConvertedExpenses,ConvertedCostAmount
FROM   Aicon.qry20102CostAllocatedMasterView

  ALter  PROCEDURE [Aicon].[StProAccountLedgerByVoucherType]       
    @VoucherType VARCHAR(50),       
    @StartDate DATETIME,      
    @EndDate DATETIME      
AS      
  
BEGIN      
  
    SET NOCOUNT ON;      
    -- Declare currency-related variables    
  
    DECLARE       
  
        @BaseCurrencyCode VARCHAR(10),      
        @BaseCurrencyImage NVARCHAR(MAX),      
        @BaseCurrencySymbole NVARCHAR(50),      
        @BaseCurrencyNote NVARCHAR(50),      
        @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode VARCHAR(50);      
    -- Get Base Currency Code from Company Table      
  
    SELECT  @BaseCurrencyCode = CurrencyType       
  
    FROM Aicon.fn_GetDefaultCompanyDetails();      
  
  
  
    -- Get Base Currency Details      
  
    SELECT TOP 1      
  
        @BaseCurrencyImage = CurrencyImage,      
        @BaseCurrencySymbole = CurrencySymbole,      
        @BaseCurrencyNote = CurrencyNote,      
        @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode = CurrencyCode      
    FROM Aicon.tbl20169CurrencyExchange       
  
    WHERE CurrencyMasterCode = @BaseCurrencyCode;      
  
    IF @VoucherType IS NOT NULL      
  
    BEGIN      
   SELECT q.VoucherNo,q.VoucherDate,q.VoucherRefNo, q.VoucherNarration, q.VoucherEnteredBy, q.VoucherEnteredOn, q.VoucherVerifiedBy, q.VoucherVerifiedOn, q.VoucherApprovedBy, q.VoucherApprovedOn, q.VoucherEntryNo, q.AccountHead,
   q.AccountHeadName, q.DrCr, q.DrAmount, q.CrAmount, q.VoucherAmountFormatted, q.EntryNarration, ag.AccountGroup, mg.MasterGroup, q.VoucherType, q.SysRemarks, q.VoucherEffectiveDate, q.SubGroupName, q.VoucherModifiedBy,
   q.VoucherModifiedOn, cex.CurrencyName AS TransactionCurrencyName, cex.CurrencySymbole AS TransactionCurrencySymbol,cex.CurrencyImage as TransactionCurrencyImage, q.VoucherAmountFormatted * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedAmount, q.DrAmount * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate,
   1) AS ConvertedDrAmount, q.CrAmount * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedCrAmount, q.CurrencyRate AS ExchangeRate, @BaseCurrencyCode AS CurrencyType, @BaseCurrencyImage AS CurrencyImage, @BaseCurrencySymbole AS
   CurrencySymbole, @BaseCurrencyNote AS CurrencyNote, @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode AS CurrencyCode
       
  
        FROM Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup mg    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups ag ON mg.MasterGroupID = ag.AccountGroupUnder    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts ca ON ag.AccountGroupID = ca.AccountGroupID    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster q ON ca.AccountID = q.AccountHead    
LEFT JOIN Aicon.tbl20169CurrencyExchange cex ON cex.CurrencyExchangeId = q.Currencyid   
    
  
        WHERE     
            q.VoucherType = @VoucherType    
            AND q.VoucherDate BETWEEN @StartDate AND DATEADD(SECOND, 86399, @EndDate)    
    END      
    ELSE      
    BEGIN      
       SELECT q.VoucherNo, q.VoucherDate, q.VoucherRefNo, q.VoucherNarration, q.VoucherEnteredBy, q.VoucherEnteredOn, q.VoucherVerifiedBy, q.VoucherVerifiedOn, q.VoucherApprovedBy, q.VoucherApprovedOn, q.VoucherEntryNo,
	   q.AccountHead, q.AccountHeadName, q.DrCr, q.DrAmount, q.CrAmount, q.VoucherAmountFormatted, q.EntryNarration, ag.AccountGroup, mg.MasterGroup, q.VoucherType, q.SysRemarks, q.VoucherEffectiveDate, q.SubGroupName,
	   q.VoucherModifiedBy, q.VoucherModifiedOn, cex.CurrencyName AS TransactionCurrencyName, cex.CurrencySymbole AS TransactionCurrencySymbol, q.VoucherAmountFormatted * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedAmount, q.DrAmount
	   * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedDrAmount, q.CrAmount * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedCrAmount, q.CurrencyRate AS ExchangeRate, @BaseCurrencyCode AS CurrencyType, @BaseCurrencyImage AS CurrencyImage,
	   @BaseCurrencySymbole AS CurrencySymbole, @BaseCurrencyNote AS CurrencyNote, @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode AS CurrencyCode,cex.CurrencyImage as TransactionCurrencyImage
	   
        FROM Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup mg    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups ag ON mg.MasterGroupID = ag.AccountGroupUnder    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts ca ON ag.AccountGroupID = ca.AccountGroupID    
        RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster q ON ca.AccountID = q.AccountHead    
LEFT JOIN Aicon.tbl20169CurrencyExchange cex ON cex.CurrencyExchangeId = q.Currencyid   
  
        WHERE     
            q.VoucherDate BETWEEN @StartDate AND DATEADD(SECOND, 86399, @EndDate)    
    END      
END   


ALTER PROCEDURE [Aicon].[StProAccountLedger]    

    @ParamAccountNo VARCHAR(50),    

    @StartDate DATETIME,   

    @EndDate DATETIME   

AS   

BEGIN   

    SET NOCOUNT ON;   
 
    DECLARE  

        @BaseCurrencyCode VARCHAR(10), 

        @BaseCurrencyImage NVARCHAR(MAX), 

        @BaseCurrencySymbole VARCHAR(50), 

        @BaseCurrencyNote VARCHAR(50), 

        @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode VARCHAR(50);
 
    -- Get Base Currency Code from Company Table 

  
    SELECT  @BaseCurrencyCode = CurrencyType       
  
    FROM Aicon.fn_GetDefaultCompanyDetails();    
 
    -- Get Base Currency Details 

    SELECT TOP 1 

        @BaseCurrencyImage = CurrencyImage, 

        @BaseCurrencySymbole = CurrencySymbole, 

        @BaseCurrencyNote = CurrencyNote, 

        @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode = CurrencyCode 

    FROM tbl20169CurrencyExchange  

    WHERE CurrencyMasterCode = @BaseCurrencyCode;
 
    -- Opening Balance Section (no CurrencyId for historical opening, so CurrencyName not applicable)

    
    SELECT '.Opening Balance' AS VoucherNo, @StartDate AS VoucherDate, '' AS VoucherRefNo, 'Opening Balance' AS VoucherNarration, '' AS VoucherEnteredBy, '' AS VoucherEnteredOn, '' AS VoucherVerifiedBy, 
	'' AS VoucherVerifiedOn, '' AS VoucherApprovedBy, '' AS VoucherApprovedOn, 0 AS VoucherEntryNo, AccountHead, AccountHeadName, '' AS DrCr, 0 AS DrAmount, 0 AS CrAmount, SUM(VoucherAmountFormatted) AS VoucherAmountFormatted,
	SUM(VoucherAmountFormatted * ISNULL(CurrencyRate, 1)) AS ConvertedAmount, 0 AS ConvertedDrAmount, 0 AS ConvertedCrAmount, 'Opening Balance' AS EntryNarration, AccountGroup, MasterGroup, 'Opening Balance' AS VoucherType,
	'Opening Balance' AS SysRemarks, '' AS VoucherModifiedBy, '' AS VoucherModifiedOn, '' AS BillNo, '' AS BillDate, '' AS BillPaidTo, '' AS BillRemarks, @StartDate AS VoucherEffectiveDate, AccountHeadArabic, 
	'' AS ReferenceNote, @BaseCurrencyCode AS CurrencyType, 1 AS ExchangeRate, @BaseCurrencyImage AS CurrencyImage, @BaseCurrencySymbole AS CurrencySymbole, @BaseCurrencyNote AS CurrencyNote, 
	@BaseCurrencyDisplayCode AS CurrencyCode, NULL AS CurrencyName, NULL AS TransactionCurrencySymbol,NULL AS TransactionCurrencyImage

    FROM Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster   

    WHERE AccountHead = @ParamAccountNo  

      AND VoucherDate < @StartDate  

      AND ISNULL(IsCalculateOpeningBalance, 0) = 1   

    GROUP BY  

        AccountHead,  

        AccountHeadName,  

        AccountGroup,  

        MasterGroup,  

        AccountHeadArabic   
 
    UNION ALL   
 
    -- Transaction Records Section

    SELECT q.VoucherNo, q.VoucherDate, q.VoucherRefNo, q.VoucherNarration, q.VoucherEnteredBy, q.VoucherEnteredOn, q.VoucherVerifiedBy, q.VoucherVerifiedOn, q.VoucherApprovedBy, q.VoucherApprovedOn, q.VoucherEntryNo, q.AccountHead, q.AccountHeadName, q.DrCr, q.DrAmount, q.CrAmount, q.VoucherAmountFormatted, q.VoucherAmountFormatted * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedAmount, q.DrAmount * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedDrAmount, q.CrAmount * ISNULL(q.CurrencyRate, 1) AS ConvertedCrAmount, q.EntryNarration, ag.AccountGroup, mg.MasterGroup, q.VoucherType, q.SysRemarks, q.VoucherModifiedBy, q.VoucherModifiedOn, q.BillNo, q.BillDate, q.BillPaidTo, q.BillRemarks, q.VoucherEffectiveDate, q.AccountHeadArabic, q.ReferenceNote, @BaseCurrencyCode AS CurrencyType, q.CurrencyRate AS ExchangeRate, @BaseCurrencyImage AS CurrencyImage, @BaseCurrencySymbole AS CurrencySymbole, @BaseCurrencyNote AS CurrencyNote, @BaseCurrencyDisplayCode AS CurrencyCode, cex.CurrencyName, cex.CurrencySymbole AS TransactionCurrencySymbol,cex.CurrencyImage as TransactionCurrencyImage
 
    FROM Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster q 

    INNER JOIN Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts ca ON ca.AccountID = q.AccountHead 

    LEFT JOIN Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups ag ON ca.AccountGroupID = ag.AccountGroupID 

    LEFT JOIN Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup mg ON ag.AccountGroupUnder = mg.MasterGroupID 

    LEFT JOIN Aicon.tbl20169CurrencyExchange cex ON q.CurrencyId = cex.CurrencyExchangeID

    WHERE q.AccountHead = @ParamAccountNo  

      AND q.VoucherDate BETWEEN @StartDate AND DATEADD(SECOND, 86399, @EndDate) 

END



--Insert Debit Note from Purchase (VAT) -23/07/25


CREATE PROCEDURE [Aicon].[sp201_85InsertDebiteNoteFromPurchase]
 
	(
 
	@PurchaseVoucherNo as varchar(20),
 
	@DebitNoteNo as varchar(20),
 
	@AddedBy varchar(20),
 
	@AddedOn datetime
 
	)
 
AS
 
BEGIN
 
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
 
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
 
SET NOCOUNT ON;

-- Insert Debit Note Master

INSERT into tbl20172VATDebitNoteMaster
 
(DebitNoteNo, DebitNoteDate, SupplierBillNo, SupplierCode, ReferenceNo,
 
AddedBy, AddedOn, CompanyID, DebitNoteEffectiveDate,
 
RevenueAccount, CostCenterCode,
 
PurchaseVoucherNo, InvoicePeriod, SupplierBillDate,  
 
BranchCode, InventoryEffectiveDate)

SELECT  @DebitNoteNo, DATEADD(dd, DATEDIFF(dd, 0, Getdate()), 0), PurchaseBillNo, SupplierCode, ReferenceNo,
 
@AddedBy, @AddedOn, CompanyID, PurchaseEffectiveDate,
 
PurchaseLedgerAccountNo, CostCenterCode,
 
PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchasePeriod, PurchaseBillDate,
 
BranchCode, InventoryEffectiveDate
 
FROM         tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster
 
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @PurchaseVoucherNo

-- Insert Debit Note Child
 
Insert Into tbl20173VATDebitNoteChild
 
(DebitNoteNo, ItemCode, UoM, QuantityDebited, UnitsToDebited, UnitRateMethod,
 
UnitRate, DetailedDescription, TaxSlabCode, TaxAmount, Discount)

SELECT  @DebitNoteNo, ItemCode, UoM, QuantityInvoiced, UnitsToBill, UnitRateMethod,
 
UnitRate, DetailedDescription, TaxSlabCode, TaxAmount, Discount
 
FROM         tbl20167VATPurchaseChild
 
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @PurchaseVoucherNo


END

CREATE TABLE Aicon.FinancialSummaryReport (
    AccountHead varchar(50),
    AccountHeadName NVARCHAR(255),
    VoucherAmountFormatted DECIMAL(18, 2),
    AccountGroup NVARCHAR(255),
    MasterGroup NVARCHAR(255),
    TransactionsFull NVARCHAR(50),
    AccountHeadArabic NVARCHAR(255),
    AccountGroupAr NVARCHAR(255),
    MasterGroupAr NVARCHAR(255)
);     
ALTER TABLE Aicon.FinancialSummaryReport ADD VoucherDate DATETIME;
ALTER TABLE Aicon.FinancialSummaryReport ADD MonthYear NVARCHAR(50);
ALTER TABLE Aicon.FinancialSummaryReport
ADD DrCr NVARCHAR(50), DrAmount DECIMAL(18,2), CrAmount DECIMAL(18,2);


INSERT INTO Aicon.FinancialSummaryReport (
    AccountHead, AccountHeadName, VoucherAmountFormatted,
    AccountGroup, MasterGroup, TransactionsFull,
    AccountHeadArabic, AccountGroupAr, MasterGroupAr,
    VoucherDate, MonthYear,
    DrCr, DrAmount, CrAmount
)
 
-- Opening Balance
SELECT  
    AccountHead,   
    AccountHeadName,   
    SUM(VoucherAmountFormatted), 
    AccountGroup, 
    MasterGroup, 
    'Opening Balance',  
    AccountHeadArabic, 
    AccountGroupAr, 
    MasterGroupAr,
    MIN(VoucherDate) AS VoucherDate,
    DATENAME(m, MIN(VoucherDate)) + ' ' + CAST(DATEPART(yyyy, MIN(VoucherDate)) AS varchar) AS MonthYear,
    NULL AS DrCr,
    SUM(DrAmount) AS DrAmount,
    SUM(CrAmount) AS CrAmount
FROM Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster 
WHERE ISNULL(IsCalculateOpeningBalance, 0) = 1  
GROUP BY 
    AccountHead, 
    AccountHeadName, 
    AccountGroup, 
    MasterGroup,  
    AccountHeadArabic, 
    AccountGroupAr, 
    MasterGroupAr
 
UNION ALL
 
-- Transactions during the period
SELECT  
    q.AccountHead, 
    q.AccountHeadName,   
    q.VoucherAmountFormatted, 
    ag.AccountGroup,  
    mg.MasterGroup,  
    CASE 
        WHEN q.VoucherAmountFormatted > 0 THEN 'Transactions Debit' 
        ELSE 'Transactions Credit' 
    END,   
    q.AccountHeadArabic,  
    q.AccountGroupAr,  
    q.MasterGroupAr,
    q.VoucherDate,
    DATENAME(m, q.VoucherEffectiveDate) + ' ' + CAST(DATEPART(yyyy, q.VoucherEffectiveDate) AS varchar) AS MonthYear,
    q.DrCr,
    q.DrAmount,
    q.CrAmount
FROM Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster AS q
    RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201ChartOfAccounts coa ON coa.AccountID = q.AccountHead
    RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201AccountGroups ag ON coa.AccountGroupID = ag.AccountGroupID
    RIGHT OUTER JOIN Aicon.tbl201MasterGroup mg ON ag.AccountGroupUnder = mg.MasterGroupID;



    CREATE TABLE Aicon.tblFcmDeviceTokens (
   Id INT IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
   UserId NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
   TenantName NVARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
   DeviceId NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
   Token NVARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
   Platform NVARCHAR(50),
   LastUpdated DATETIME DEFAULT GETDATE()
);

-- Add InvoiceCurrencyCode column if it does not exist

    SELECT 1 FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS 
    WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'Aicon' 
      AND TABLE_NAME = 'tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster' 
      AND COLUMN_NAME = 'InvoiceCurrencyCode'
)
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE [Aicon].tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster  
    ADD InvoiceCurrencyCode VARCHAR(10);
END

-- Add ExchangeRate column if it does not exist
IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS 
    WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'Aicon' 
      AND TABLE_NAME = 'tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster' 
      AND COLUMN_NAME = 'ExchangeRate'
)
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE [Aicon].tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster  
    ADD ExchangeRate DECIMAL(19,4);
END

-- Add TaxCurrencyCode column if it does not exist
IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM INFORMATION_SCHEMA.COLUMNS 
    WHERE TABLE_SCHEMA = 'Aicon' 
      AND TABLE_NAME = 'tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster' 
      AND COLUMN_NAME = 'TaxCurrencyCode'
)
BEGIN
    ALTER TABLE [Aicon].tbl20166VATPurchaseMaster  
    ADD TaxCurrencyCode VARCHAR(10);
END

FROM   [Aicon].qry201_606VATInvoiceRegisterMain LEFT OUTER JOIN
             [Aicon].tbl20169CurrencyExchange ON [Aicon].qry201_606VATInvoiceRegisterMain.Currency = [Aicon].tbl20169CurrencyExchange.CurrencyExchangeID LEFT OUTER JOIN
             [Aicon].qry90134eInvoice_LatestICVwtDetails ON [Aicon].qry201_606VATInvoiceRegisterMain.InvoiceNo = [Aicon].qry90134eInvoice_LatestICVwtDetails.eInvoiceNo LEFT OUTER JOIN
             [Aicon].tbl201ChartOfAccounts ON [Aicon].qry201_606VATInvoiceRegisterMain.RevenueAccount = [Aicon].tbl201ChartOfAccounts.AccountID

ALTER PROCEDURE [Aicon].[sp201_72InsertVATPurchasetoVoucher_BHD] 
	(
	@VoucherNo as varchar(50),
	@JustAddedVoucherEntryNoSubLedger as int,
	@JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc as int,
	@IsCashOrBankAccount as bit,
	@IsExpensesAccount as bit
	)
AS
BEGIN
	-- SET NOCOUNT ON added to prevent extra result sets from
	-- interfering with SELECT statements.
SET NOCOUNT ON;

-- Insert to Voucher Master
INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201VoucherMaster(
VoucherNo, VoucherDate, VoucherRefNo, VoucherNarration, VoucherEnteredBy, VoucherEnteredOn, 
VoucherType, VoucherEffectiveDate, InvoiceDueDate, InvoiceSubmittedDate, Currencyid,CurrencyRate,basecurrencyid )

SELECT PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseVoucherDate, PurchaseBillNo, 'Bill from ' + SupplierName + ' on account of ' + ExpensesAccountName + '. (' + Isnull(Remarks, '') + ')', 
AddedBy, AddedOn, 'Purchases', PurchaseEffectiveDate, PurchaseDueDate, SupplierInvRecdOn,InvoiceCurrencyCode,ExchangeRate,TaxCurrencyCode
From [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseMaster 
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo

------------------------------------------



-- Insert to Voucher Child Entry (Debit Fields)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201VoucherEntry(
VoucherNo, AccountHead, VoucherAmount, DrCr, AddedBy, AddedOn, EntryNarration,
SysRemarks)

SELECT PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseLedgerAccountNo, TotalInvoiceAmount_BHD, 'Dr', AddedBy, AddedOn,
'Bill from ' + SupplierName + ' on account of ' + ExpensesAccountName + '. (' + Isnull(Remarks, '') + ')', SupplierName
FROM          [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseChild
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo

select @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc = Scope_Identity()

-- Insert to Voucher Child Entry (VAT Amount)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201VoucherEntry(
VoucherNo, AccountHead, VoucherAmount, DrCr, AddedBy, AddedOn, EntryNarration,
SysRemarks)

SELECT PurchaseVoucherNo, 'S00100', TotalTaxAmount_BHD, 'Dr', AddedBy, AddedOn,
'Bill from ' + SupplierName + ' on account of ' + ExpensesAccountName + '. (' + Isnull(Remarks, '') + ')', SupplierName
FROM         [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseChild
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo



---Insert Credit Account to Voucher Entry

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201VoucherEntry(
VoucherNo, AccountHead, VoucherAmount, DrCr, AddedBy, AddedOn, EntryNarration,
SysRemarks)

SELECT PurchaseVoucherNo, SupplierCode, TotalWithTax_BHD, 'Cr', AddedBy, AddedOn,
'Bill from ' + SupplierName + ' on account of ' + ExpensesAccountName + '. (' + Isnull(Remarks, '') + ')' , ExpensesAccountName
FROM         [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseChild
Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo

select @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoSubLedger = Scope_Identity()


----Insert to SubLedger Master
--Check If the Account is Cash/Bank Account ... then skip this step

IF (@IsCashOrBankAccount = 0)
	BEGIN
		INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201SubLedgerMaster(
		ReferenceType, VoucherEntryNo, ReferenceNo, AccountNo, 
		Amount, DrCr, AddedBy, AddedOn, VoucherNo, RetentionAmount)

		SELECT 
		'New Reference', @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoSubLedger, PurchaseVoucherNo, SupplierCode,
		TotalWithTax_BHD, 'Cr', AddedBy, AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo, RetentionAmount
		FROM         [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseChild
		Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	END


------Insert to Cost Allocation Master

IF (@IsExpensesAccount = 1)
	BEGIN
	INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl201CostAllocationMaster(
	VoucherEntryID, CostAllocationUnitID, CostAllocDrCr, AmountAllocated, EffectiveDate, CostAllocRemarks,
	EnteredBy, EnteredOn, VoucherNo)

	SELECT @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc, CostCenterCode, 'Dr', TotalInvoiceAmount_BHD, PurchaseEffectiveDate, 'VAT Purchase Bill',
	AddedBy,  AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo
	FROM         [Aicon].qry201_708VATtoPurchaseChild
	Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	
	END



-- Insert Property Allocation Master (Debit Amounts)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl20122PropertyAllocationMaster(
	VoucherEntryID, PropertyNo, PropertyAllocDrCr, AmountAllocated, EffectiveDate, PropertyAllocRemarks,
	EnteredBy, EnteredOn, VoucherNo, LedgerAccountNo)

	SELECT @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc, PropertyNo, 'Dr', TotalCalcLineAmount, PurchaseEffectiveDate, 'VAT Purchase Bill',
	AddedBy,  AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseLedgerAccountNo
	FROM         [Aicon].qry201_725VATPurchaseChildTotal_ForPropertyCost
	Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	
		
-- Insert Property Allocation Master (Credit Amounts)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl20122PropertyAllocationMaster(
	VoucherEntryID, PropertyNo, PropertyAllocDrCr, AmountAllocated, EffectiveDate, PropertyAllocRemarks,
	EnteredBy, EnteredOn, VoucherNo, LedgerAccountNo)

	SELECT @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc, PropertyNo, 'Cr',  - TotalCalcLineAmount AS TotalCalcLineAmount, PurchaseEffectiveDate, 'VAT Purchase Bill',
	AddedBy,  AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseLedgerAccountNo
	FROM         [Aicon].qry201_725VATPurchaseChildTotal_ForPropertyCostCredits
	Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	----


-- Insert Employee Allocation Master (Debit Amounts)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster(
	VoucherEntryID, EmployeeNo, EmpAllocDrCr, AmountAllocated, EffectiveDate, CostAllocRemarks,
	EnteredBy, EnteredOn, VoucherNo, LedgerAccountNo)

	SELECT @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc, EmployeeNo, 'Dr', TotalCalcLineAmount, PurchaseEffectiveDate, 'VAT Purchase Bill',
	AddedBy,  AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseLedgerAccountNo
	FROM         [Aicon].qry201_725VATPurchaseChildTotal_ForEmployeeCost
	Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	


-- Insert Employee Allocation Master (Credit Amounts)

INSERT INTO [Aicon].tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster(
	VoucherEntryID, EmployeeNo, EmpAllocDrCr, AmountAllocated, EffectiveDate, CostAllocRemarks,
	EnteredBy, EnteredOn, VoucherNo, LedgerAccountNo)

	SELECT @JustAddedVoucherEntryNoCostAlloc, EmployeeNo, 'Cr',  - TotalCalcLineAmount AS TotalCalcLineAmount, PurchaseEffectiveDate, 'VAT Purchase Bill',
	AddedBy,  AddedOn, PurchaseVoucherNo, PurchaseLedgerAccountNo
	FROM         [Aicon].qry201_725VATPurchaseChildTotal_ForEmployeeCostCredits
	Where PurchaseVoucherNo = @VoucherNo
	
END

RFQAbbrv = 'SR-RFQ' ,
SalesOrderAbbrv = 'SR-SO', 
PurchaseOrderAbbrv = 'SR-PO',
MaterialReceiptAbbrv = 'SR-MPR', 
DeliveryNoteAbbrv = 'SR-DN',
WHERE CompanyID =2

UPDATE Sudhir.tbl901CompanyDetails02
SET NoOfDigitsToInventoryQuotation = 5
WHERE CompanyID = 2;

                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ClientCode, Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientName, Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientNameAr, Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientAddress, 
                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.StoreCode, Aicon.tbl600_01StoreMaster.StoreName, Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster.TypeOfRequest, Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster.ModeOfRequest, 
                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.MPRRemarks, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.GSCode, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.GSDescrpition, 
                      Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.QtyRequested, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.UnitRateMethod, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.UnitCode, 
                      Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.UnitType, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.UnitDesc, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemRemarks,

                      Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.DeliveryPeriod, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemSize, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemPartNo, 
                      Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemBrand, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemMake, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.PurposeOfRequest, 
                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.Priority, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ExpectedDate, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.Remarks, 
                      CASE WHEN Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ClientCode IS NOT NULL 
                      THEN Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientName ELSE (CASE WHEN Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ProjectMasterCode IS NOT NULL 
                      THEN Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectDescription ELSE (CASE WHEN Aicon.tbl101Departments.DepartmentCode IS NOT NULL 
                      THEN Aicon.tbl101Departments.Department ELSE (CASE WHEN Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.StoreCode IS NOT NULL THEN Aicon.tbl600_01StoreMaster.StoreName ELSE '' END) END) END) 
                      END AS RequestedNameRef, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemReqPurpose, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemReqPriority, 
                      Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ItemReqExpectedDate, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.ExpectedUnitRate, Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild.LineTotal, 
                      Aicon.qry606_07PurchaseRequestWithTotalTax.ExpectedTaxAmount, Aicon.qry606_07PurchaseRequestWithTotalTax.ExpectedTotalAmount, 
                      Aicon.qry606_07PurchaseRequestWithTotalTax.TotalRequestCost, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.CostCenterText, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ProjectMasterCode, 
                      Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectDescription, Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectID, Aicon.tbl600_02RequestedBy.RequestedBy, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.RequesterContact, 
                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.RequesterContactEmail, Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.RequestedBy AS RequestingPersonName,

                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_2.SignatureImage AS MPR_PreparedBySignatoryImage, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_2.SignatoryMobile1 AS MPR_PreparedBySignatoryMobile1, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_2.SignatoryMobile2 AS MPR_PreparedBySignatoryMobile2, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_2.SignatoryNameAr AS MPR_PreparedBySignatoryNameAr, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_2.SignatoryPositionAr AS MPR_PreparedBySignatoryPositionAr, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryName AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryName, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryPosition AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryPosition, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryContact AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryContact, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryEmail AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryEmail, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatureImage AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryImage, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryMobile1 AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryMobile1, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryMobile2 AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryMobile2, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryNameAr AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryNameAr, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories.SignatoryPositionAr AS MPR_VerifiedBySignatoryPositionAr,

                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_1.SignatureImage AS MPR_ApprovedBySignatoryImage, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_1.SignatoryMobile1 AS MPR_ApprovedBySignatoryMobile1, 
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_1.SignatoryMobile2 AS MPR_ApprovedBySignatoryMobile2, Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_1.SignatoryNameAr AS MPR_ApprovedBySignatoryNameAr,

FROM         Aicon.qry606_02PurchaseRequestChild RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories AS Aicon.tbl90104DocumentSignatories_1 RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl606_04PurchaseRequestProjectSubUnits RIGHT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster ON Aicon.tbl606_04PurchaseRequestProjectSubUnits.ProjectSubUnitCode = Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ProjectSubUnitCode ON

                      Aicon.tbl600_02RequestedBy ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.TypeOfMPR = Aicon.tbl600_02RequestedBy.RequestedByCode ON 
                      Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectID = Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ProjectMasterCode LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ClientCode = Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientCode LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.qry606_07PurchaseRequestWithTotalTax ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.MPRNo = Aicon.qry606_07PurchaseRequestWithTotalTax.MPRNo LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl101Departments ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.DepartmentID = Aicon.tbl101Departments.DepartmentCode LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.ModeOfRequest = Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster.ModeOfRequestID ON 
                      Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster.TypeOfRequestID = Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.TypeOfRequest LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.tbl600_01StoreMaster ON Aicon.tbl606_01PurchaseRequestMaster.StoreCode = Aicon.tbl600_01StoreMaster.StoreID ON

                      Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.RFQSummary, Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.SignatoryName, Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.SignatoryPosition, Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.SignatoryContact, 
                      Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.SignatoryEmail, Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.SignatureImage, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.RFQChildSlNo, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.IsWonForPO, 
                      Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.IsWonUpdatedBy, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.IsWonUpdatedDate, Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.Project, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.PlanNo, 
                      Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.DeliveryPeriod AS Expr1, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.ItemSize, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.ItemPartNo, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.ItemBrand, Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.ItemMake,

FROM         Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster LEFT OUTER JOIN
                      Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild ON Aicon.qry607_04RFQViewMaster.RFQNo = Aicon.qry607_02RFQChild.RFQNo
GO




-------------------------------------------------------
-- tbl201SubLedgerMaster
IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.columns 
    WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster') 
      AND name = 'CurrencyRate'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster
ADD CurrencyRate DECIMAL(18, 4) NULL 
    CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201SubLedgerMaster_CurrencyRate DEFAULT 1.00;

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.columns 
    WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster') 
      AND name = 'BaseCurrency'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster
ADD BaseCurrency INT NOT NULL 
    CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201SubLedgerMaster_BaseCurrency DEFAULT 1;

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.columns 
    WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster') 
      AND name = 'Currency'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster
ADD Currency INT NOT NULL 
    CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201SubLedgerMaster_Currency DEFAULT 1;

UPDATE Aicon.tbl201SubLedgerMaster
SET CurrencyRate  = ISNULL(CurrencyRate, 1.00),
    BaseCurrency  = ISNULL(BaseCurrency, 1),
    Currency      = ISNULL(Currency, 1)
WHERE CurrencyRate IS NULL
   OR BaseCurrency IS NULL
   OR Currency IS NULL;

-------------------------------------------------------
-- tbl201CostAllocationMaster
IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.columns 
    WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster') 
      AND name = 'CurrencyRate'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster
ADD CurrencyRate DECIMAL(18, 4) NULL;

UPDATE Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster
SET CurrencyRate = 1.00
WHERE CurrencyRate IS NULL;

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.default_constraints dc
    JOIN sys.columns c 
      ON dc.parent_object_id = c.object_id 
     AND dc.parent_column_id = c.column_id
    WHERE dc.parent_object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster') 
      AND c.name = 'CurrencyRate'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster
ADD CONSTRAINT DF_tbl201CostAllocationMaster_CurrencyRate DEFAULT 1.00 FOR CurrencyRate;

)

-- tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster
IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM sys.columns 
    WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID('Aicon.tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster') 
      AND name = 'CurrencyRate'
)
ALTER TABLE Aicon.tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster
ADD CurrencyRate DECIMAL(18, 4) NULL 
    CONSTRAINT DF_tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster_CurrencyRate DEFAULT 1.00;

    ALTER VIEW [Aicon].[qry201RptVoucherWithCost]
AS
SELECT Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.CostAllocationID, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.VoucherEntryID, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationUnits.CostAllocationUnit, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationUnits.CostAllocationGroup, 
                  Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.CostAllocDrCr, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.AmountAllocated, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.CostAllocRemarks, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster.AccountHeadName, 
                  Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster.VoucherNo, Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster.VoucherAmountFormatted, Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.CurrencyRate
FROM     Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster LEFT OUTER JOIN
                  Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster ON Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.VoucherEntryID = Aicon.qry201MainVoucherEntriesWithMaster.VoucherEntryNo LEFT OUTER JOIN
                  Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationUnits ON Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationMaster.CostAllocationUnitID = Aicon.tbl201CostAllocationUnits.CostAllocationUnitID
    CONSTRAINT DF_tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster_CurrencyRate DEFAULT 1.00;

UPDATE Aicon.tbl20104EmployeeAllocationMaster
SET CurrencyRate = 1.00
WHERE CurrencyRate IS NULL;

ALTER COLUMN currencyrate DECIMAL(18,4);

=======Equipment Enquiry=======
ALTER VIEW [Aicon].[qry406_01PropertyRequestView]
AS
SELECT     Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.EqiupmentRequestNo, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.RequestDate, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.TypeOfMPR, 
Aicon.tbl40135_PropertyRequestTypes.RequestType, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ClientCode, Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientName, Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster.ModeOfRequestID, 
Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster.ModeOfRequest, Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster.TypeOfRequestID, Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster.TypeOfRequest, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.IsApproved, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.PreparedBy, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.PreparedOn, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ApprovedBy, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ApprovedOn, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.AddedBy, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.AddedOn, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ModifiedBy, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ModifiedOn, Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails.CompanyID, 
Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails.CompanyName, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.RequestedBy, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.RequesterContact, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.RequesterContactEmail, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.RequestSignatory, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ClientRefNo, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.PurposeOfRequest, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.Priority, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ExpectedDate, 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.Remarks, Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ProjectMasterCode, Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectDescription, 
Aicon.qry406_03EquipmentRequestChildWtTotal.TotalRequestCost, Aicon.qry406_03EquipmentRequestChildWtTotal.NoOfItems,Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.IsVerified,
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.IsSubmitted,Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.SubmittedBy,Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.VerifiedBy,
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.SubmittedOn,Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.VerifiedOn,Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ERverifiedSign,
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ERapprovedSign
FROM   Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.qry406_03EquipmentRequestChildWtTotal ON 
Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.EqiupmentRequestNo = Aicon.qry406_03EquipmentRequestChildWtTotal.EquipmentRequestNo LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.CompanyBranch = Aicon.tbl901CompanyDetails.CompanyID LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.TypeOfRequest = Aicon.tbl30104TypeOfRequestMaster.TypeOfRequestID LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ModeOfRequest = Aicon.tbl30103ModeOfRequestMaster.ModeOfRequestID LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ProjectMasterCode = Aicon.tbl700_01ProjectMaster.ProjectID LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.ClientCode = Aicon.tbl30101ClientMaster.ClientCode LEFT OUTER JOIN
Aicon.tbl40135_PropertyRequestTypes ON Aicon.tbl40136_PropertyRequestMaster.TypeOfMPR = Aicon.tbl40135_PropertyRequestTypes.EquipmentRequestTypeCode
GO


ALTER VIEW [Aicon].[qry406_04PropertyRequestViewMaster]
AS
SELECT     EqiupmentRequestNo, RequestDate, TypeOfMPR, RequestType, ClientCode, ClientName, ModeOfRequestID, ModeOfRequest, TypeOfRequestID, TypeOfRequest, IsApproved, PreparedBy, 
ERapprovedSign,ERverifiedSign,VerifiedOn,SubmittedOn,VerifiedBy,SubmittedBy,IsVerified,IsSubmitted,
PreparedOn, ApprovedBy, ApprovedOn, AddedBy, AddedOn, ModifiedBy, ModifiedOn, CompanyID, CompanyName, RequestedBy, RequesterContact, RequesterContactEmail, RequestSignatory, 
ClientRefNo, PurposeOfRequest, Priority, ExpectedDate, Remarks, ProjectMasterCode, ProjectDescription, TotalRequestCost, NoOfItems
FROM   Aicon.qry406_01PropertyRequestView
GO
